> **SCOPE: GENERIC** (currency/timeframe-agnostic). Per-key results live in `<PAIR>_RESULTS.md` / `sweeps/`. See REPO_MAP.md.

# CORPUS LEVER INVENTORY — every testable lever mined from /home/sean/git/academic-papers (2026-06-03)

Exhaustive extraction of **550 levers** from the distilled corpus (`_CORPUS_INDEX.md` + `_extracted_levers.json`, 370 papers), via an 11-agent workflow. Each: methodology · 5m test-plan · target/side · sign-invariance · data · status · falsifier · prior. **Status legend:** UNTESTED · tested-killed · tested-certified · tested-marginal · subsumed (Tier-1 dominated) · external-blocked (needs funded data). This documents EXISTENCE+METHODOLOGY for the whole literature; testable UNTESTED on-disk rows are queued into `SWEEP_MATRIX.md` Tier-N + `sweeps/<key>_backlog.md`.

## Summary
- by status: {'tested-killed': 46, 'UNTESTED': 363, 'subsumed': 102, 'external-blocked': 16, 'tested-certified': 11, 'tested-marginal': 12}

- by target: {'direction': 135, 'magnitude': 6, 'gate': 121, 'feature': 134, 'sizing': 24, 'labeling': 36, 'validation': 94}
